Conclusion: LLMs能够生成有效的HTN规划搜索启发式，接近当前最优水平，并大幅降低搜索开销，表明该方法在层次化规划中具有潜力。

Abstract: HTN planning is a variation of classical planning where, instead of searching for a linear sequence of actions, an algorithm decomposes higher-level tasks using a method library until only executable actions remain. On one hand, this allows one to introduce domain knowledge that can speed up the search for a solution through the method library. On the other hand, it creates challenges that go beyond those of classical state-space search. While recent research produced a number of heuristics and novel algorithms that speed up HTN planning, these heuristics are not yet as informative as those available in classical planning algorithms. We investigate whether large language models (LLMs) can generate effective search heuristics for HTN planning, extending the methodology of Corrêa, Pereira, and Seipp (2025) from classical to hierarchical planning. Using the Pytrich planner on six standard total-order HTN benchmark domains, we evaluate heuristics generated by nine LLMs under domain-specific prompting and compare them against the TDG and LMCount domain-independent baselines and the PANDA planner. Our results show that LLM-generated heuristics nearly match the coverage of the best available HTN planner, while substantially reducing search effort on 83% of shared problems.

</details>


### [254] [Online Goal Recognition using Path Signature and Dynamic Time Warping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07736)
*Douglas Tesch,Nathan Gavenski,Leonardo Amado,Odinaldo Rodrigues,Felipe Meneguzzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于路径签名（path signatures）的在线目标识别方法，利用粗糙路径理论的紧凑表达有效编码轨迹特征，在预测精度和在线规划效率上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线目标识别在连续域中面临两大挑战：高效编码大规模轨迹和有效比较轨迹。现有方法常忽视其他领域成熟的编码技术，导致性能受限。

Method: 引入路径签名（path signatures）作为轨迹的紧凑表达，利用粗糙路径理论高效捕获轨迹的关键语义特征，从而实现更有意义的轨迹比较。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在预测精度和在线规划效率上持续优于现有技术，同时在离线场景下保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 路径签名是一种有效的在线目标识别工具，能够兼顾编码效率与比较质量，在在线场景下具有显著优势。

Abstract: Online goal recognition in continuous domains poses two central challenges: efficiently encoding large trajectories and effectively comparing them. Recent work addresses these challenges by using custom state-space representations and metrics to compare observations against hypotheses. However, these approaches often overlook well-established encoding techniques used in other domains that offer substantial advantages. This paper introduces a novel method for online goal recognition that leverages path signatures, a compact, expressive representation of rough path theory that efficiently captures key semantic features of trajectories, enabling more meaningful comparisons between them. Experiments show that our method consistently outperforms the state of the art in predictive accuracy and online planning efficiency, while remaining competitive offline.

</details>


### [255] [Alternating Target-Path Planning for Scalable Multi-Agent Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07744)
*Yu Kumagai,Keisuke Okumura*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种迭代优化框架，将目标分配与路径规划解耦，利用快速次优MAPF求解器（如LaCAM）通过反馈驱动重分配，在可接受解质量下大幅提升TAPF问题的可扩展性，超越了基于CBS的现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TAPF方法依赖CBS，将目标分配与路径规划紧密耦合，导致计算密集、可扩展性差，难以应用于大规模实际场景。

Method: 提出迭代优化框架：在给定时间预算内反复执行(1)基于当前目标分配求解MAPF(使用LaCAM等快速次优求解器)，(2)通过MAPF反馈识别瓶颈智能体，(3)根据反馈优化目标分配。

Result: 反馈驱动的重分配循环效果显著，框架可扩展性远超现有基于CBS的求解器，同时保持可接受的解质量。

Conclusion: 该工作是向实用大规模TAPF迈出的坚实一步，适用于现实场景。

Abstract: The concurrent target assignment and pathfinding (TAPF) problem extends multi-agent pathfinding (MAPF) by asking planners to allocate distinct targets and collision-free paths to agents. Prior work on TAPF has relied exclusively on Conflict-Based Search (CBS), which tightly couples target assignment and pathfinding, resulting in compute-intensive, non-scalable solutions. In contrast, we propose an iterative refinement framework that decouples target assignment from pathfinding. Our framework builds on modern, fast, suboptimal MAPF solvers, such as LaCAM. Specifically, within a given time budget, it repeatedly solves MAPF for the current target assignment, identifies bottleneck agents via MAPF feedback, and refines the assignment. Empirical results show that feedback-driven reassignment loop is effective, enabling our framework to scale well beyond the reach of the state-of-the-art CBS-based solver while maintaining decent solution quality. This represents a solid step toward practical, large scale TAPF suitable for real-world setups.

</details>


### [256] [RuleSafe-VL: Evaluating Rule-Conditioned Decision Reasoning in Vision-Language Content Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07760)
*Zhifeng Lu,Dianyuan Wang,Yuhu Shang,Zhenbo Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RuleSafe-VL基准，用于评估视觉语言模型在内容审核中的规则条件决策推理能力。该基准包含93条原子规则、92种规则关系类型及2,166个敏感图文案例，通过四项诊断任务分解审核决策链。实验表明，规则关系恢复是主要瓶颈，最佳模型仅达到64.8 Macro-F1，部分安全导向模型低于7 Macro-F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态安全基准仅将审核简化为最终标签匹配，无法测试模型是否正确应用规则，高分数可能源于对表面线索的利用而非真正的政策理解。

Method: 从公开平台审核政策中形式化93条原子规则和92种规则关系类型，构建2,166个上下文敏感图文案例。设计四项诊断任务：识别激活规则、恢复规则交互、判断决策充分性、在补充上下文后解决结果。

Result: 在10个前沿/开源/安全导向VLM上测试，规则关系恢复任务最佳模型仅64.8 Macro-F1，部分安全导向模型低于7 Macro-F1；决策状态预测最高64.5 Macro-F1。

Conclusion: RuleSafe-VL将审核评估从最终标签评分转向规则条件决策推理的诊断性评估，揭示了现有模型在规则推理方面的严重不足。

Abstract: Platform content moderation applies explicit policy rules and context-dependent conditions to decide whether user content is allowed, restricted, or removed. A correct moderation outcome must therefore depend on which rules a case activates, how those rules interact, and whether the available evidence is sufficient. Current multimodal safety benchmarks largely reduce moderation to matching predefined final labels, leaving this underlying rule structure untested. As a result, a high benchmark score reveals little about whether a model applies the policy correctly or arrives at the correct label through superficial cues. To evaluate this rule-governed process, we introduce RuleSafe-VL, a benchmark for rule-conditioned decision reasoning in vision-language content moderation. Derived from publicly available platform moderation policies, RuleSafe-VL formalizes 93 atomic rules and 92 typed rule relations, yielding 2,166 context-sensitive image-text cases across three high-risk policy families. Its four diagnostic tasks decompose moderation into a rule-conditioned decision chain. They identify activated rules, recover rule interactions, judge decision sufficiency, and resolve outcomes once missing context is supplied. Experiments on 10 frontier, open-source, and safety-oriented VLMs reveal rule-relation recovery as the dominant bottleneck, where the best model reaches only 64.8 Macro-F1 and some safety-oriented models fall below 7 Macro-F1. Decision-state prediction also remains unreliable, peaking at 64.5 Macro-F1. RuleSafe-VL shifts moderation evaluation from final-label scoring toward diagnostic assessment of rule-conditioned decision reasoning.

</details>


### [257] [Exact Regular-Constrained Variable-Order Markov Generation via Sparse Context-State Belief Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07839)
*François Pachet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将变量阶马尔可夫模型与正则约束通过信念传播（BP）进行精确结合的方法，通过用观测上下文状态替代一阶马尔可夫状态并与约束自动机做标准乘积，解决了变量阶生成器与一阶约束层之间的状态空间不匹配问题，实现了线性或多项式时间内的精确推理，并支持可逆数据增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有方法仅支持一阶马尔可夫链与正则约束的精确结合（通过BP），但变量阶/回退模型在生成时依赖最长可用后缀，而一阶约束层会合并变量阶生成器有意保持区分的不同历史状态，导致分布计算错误。因此需要为变量阶模型建立正确的状态空间以适配现有BP-正则框架。

Method: 将变量阶马尔可夫模型的观测上下文状态（而非一阶马尔可夫状态）与正则约束的自动机状态做标准乘积，构建稀疏乘积图。推理时在该乘积图上运行信念传播，对于固定的训练上下文图和自动机，推理复杂度与序列视界长度呈线性关系；一般情况下与可达乘积边数呈多项式关系。

Result: 该方法在不展开到所有K元组的前提下，获得了变量阶分布受正则约束下的正确条件分布。推理复杂度：固定上下文图和自动机时线性于序列长度，一般情形多项式于可达乘积边数。同时支持通过逆计数查找实现可逆数据增强，效果匹配物化转置增强但无需存储变换后的语料库。

Conclusion: 变量阶马尔可夫模型可以精确地与正则约束结合，关键在于用观测上下文状态替代一阶状态进行乘积构造。同时需注意将生成时的回退策略（如单例避免）的随机语义显式化，否则不能声称精确性。

Abstract: Variable-order Markov models generate sequences over a finite alphabet by conditioning each symbol on the longest available suffix of the generated history. Regular constraints, by contrast, describe finite-horizon control requirements by an automaton: fixed positions, forced endings, metrical patterns, and forbidden copied fragments are all special cases. Existing exact methods already handle regular constraints with belief propagation for first-order Markov chains. The contribution here is the variable-order extension: identifying the state space on which the existing BP-regular machinery must be run when the generator is a variable-order/backoff model. A first-order constraint layer can enforce useful support conditions, but it computes future mass after merging histories that a variable-order generator deliberately keeps distinct. We formalize this mismatch and give the sparse construction obtained by replacing the first-order Markov state with the observed context state, then taking the standard product with the regular constraint automaton. For a fixed trained context graph and automaton, inference is linear in the sequence horizon; in general it is polynomial in the number of reachable product edges. This gives the correct variable-order distribution conditioned on regular constraints without expanding to all K-tuples. The same finite-source interface supports reversible data augmentation by inverse count lookup, matching materialized transposition augmentation without storing transformed corpora. We also separate exact BP inference from generation-time backoff policies, such as singleton avoidance, whose stochastic semantics must be made explicit if exactness is claimed.

</details>


### [258] [TraceFix: Repairing Agent Coordination Protocols with TLA+ Counterexamples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07935)
*Shuren Xia,Qiwei Li,Taqiya Ehsan,Jorge Ortiz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TraceFix验证优先流水线，利用TLA+模型检查器验证LLM多智能体协调协议，在48个任务中全部通过验证，62.5%一次通过，运行时监控将死锁/活锁从31.1%降至14.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体协调中存在协议错误（死锁、活锁等），现有方法缺乏形式化验证保障，需要一种验证优先的方法来确保协调协议的正确性。

Method: 从任务描述合成协议拓扑IR，生成PlusCal协调逻辑，利用TLC反例迭代修复协议直至验证通过，将验证后的进程体编译为智能体提示，运行时监控拒绝越拓扑操作。

Result: 48个任务全部通过TLC验证，62.5%首次通过，最多4次修复迭代；状态空间跨6个数量级，验证均在60秒内完成；3456次运行时对比显示拓扑监控执行达89.4%平均完成率；TLC验证协议将DL/LL从31.1%降至14.1%。

Conclusion: 验证优先的协议合成与运行时监控能显著提升LLM多智能体协调的正确性和鲁棒性，在模型能力降低时性能退化更慢。

Abstract: We present TraceFix, a verification-first pipeline for Large Language Model (LLM) multi-agent coordination. An agent synthesizes a protocol topology as a structured intermediate representation (IR) from a task description, generates PlusCal coordination logic, and iteratively repairs the protocol using counterexamples from the TLA+ model checker (TLC) until verification succeeds. Verified process bodies are compiled into per-agent system prompts and executed under a runtime monitor that rejects out-of-topology coordination operations. On 48 tasks spanning 16 scenario families, all tasks reach full TLC verification; 62.5% pass on the first attempt and none requires more than four repair iterations. State spaces span six orders of magnitude yet verification completes in under 60 s for every task. A 3,456-run runtime comparison shows that topology-monitored execution achieves the highest task completion (89.4% average, 81.5% full) and that runtimes using the verified protocol degrade at roughly half the rate of prompt-only and chat-only baselines when model capability is reduced. A paired ablation under a fixed runtime shows that TLC-verified protocols cut deadlock/livelock (DL/LL) from 31.1% to 14.1%, with the largest separation under fault injection.

</details>


### [259] [The Limits of AI-Driven Allocation: Optimal Screening under Aleatoric Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07979)
*Santiago Cortes-Gomez,Mateo Dulce Rubio,Carlos Patino,Bryan Wilder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究如何最优组合传统筛查（直接观察真实脆弱性）与算法预测（基于风险评分）的两阶段资源分配框架。最优策略是对算法分配边缘的单元进行筛查，直接针对最高风险单元，且当人群中的偶然不确定性增加时，筛查效率增益更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法目标分配虽比传统筛查更便宜快捷，但偶然不确定性导致的资源错配无法消除。作者希望探索如何将两种方法最优结合，以在固定预算下减少错配。

Method: 提出两阶段分配框架：第一阶段（筛查）对部分单元直接观察真实结果，第二阶段（算法分配）基于预测风险分配资源。理论上推导最优策略，并在哥伦比亚的社会保障和排雷数据上实证验证。

Result: 最优策略是筛查算法分配决策边界上的单元，同时对最高风险单元直接分配。筛查与算法分配在效率上呈现互补或替代关系：人群中偶然不确定性越大，筛查带来的效率增益越大。

Conclusion: 在固定覆盖预算下，混合使用筛查和算法目标分配可以提升资源分配效率，且筛查应聚焦于算法决策最不确定的边缘单元。

Abstract: The rise of machine learning has shifted targeted resource allocation in policy and humanitarian settings toward algorithmic targeting based on predicted risk scores. This approach is typically cheaper and faster than traditional screening procedures that directly observe the latent vulnerability status through physical verification. Yet, even access to the true conditional vulnerability probability cannot eliminate misallocation: aleatoric uncertainty over individual vulnerability status is irreducible, and probabilistic targeting inevitably misallocates some resources. In this work we study how screening and algorithmic targeting should be optimally combined in a two-stage allocation framework where a screening stage observes true outcomes for a subset of units before a final allocation stage assigns the resource under a fixed coverage budget. We show that the optimal strategy screens units at the margin of algorithmic allocation, while directly targeting the highest-risk units. Furthermore, we empirically characterize when screening and algorithmic targeting act as complements or substitutes: efficiency gains from screening grow as the aleatoric uncertainty in the population increases. We illustrate our framework with applications in income-based social protection programs and humanitarian demining in Colombia, where the tension between screening costs and allocation efficiency is operationally consequential.

</details>


### [260] [Abductive Reasoning with Probabilistic Commonsense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08011)
*Joseph Cotnareanu,Chiara Roverato,Han Zhou,Didier Chetelat,Yingxue Zhang,Mark Coates*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对大型语言模型在神经符号推理中因常识知识差异导致的问题，提出概率框架PACS，通过采样个体不同的常识信念并聚合结论，在多项基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经符号方法依赖LLM补充常识假设，但隐含假设常识事实是普遍一致的，忽视了常识信念因人而异的现实，导致推理结果不符合真实世界中个体间的常识差异。

Method: 提出概率溯因常识推理（PACS）算法：利用LLM与形式化求解器对个体不同的常识信念进行采样生成证明，并将多个样本的结论进行概率聚合，从而判断多数人对某一陈述的真假判定。

Result: 在多个基准上，PACS在性能上优于链式思维推理、先前的神经符号方法以及基于搜索的方法。

Conclusion: 明确建模个体间常识信念的变异性能够提升神经符号推理框架的鲁棒性和准确性，概率聚合策略是处理常识分歧的有效手段。

Abstract: Recent efforts to improve the reasoning abilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) have focused on integrating formal logic solvers within neurosymbolic frameworks. A key challenge is that formal solvers lack commonsense world knowledge, preventing them from making reasoning steps that humans find obvious. Prior methods address this by using LLMs to supply missing commonsense assumptions, but these approaches implicitly assume universal agreement on such commonsense facts. In reality, commonsense beliefs vary across individuals. We propose a probabilistic framework for abductive commonsense reasoning that explicitly models this variation, aiming to determine whether most people would judge a statement as true or false. We introduce Probabilistic Abductive CommonSense (PACS), a novel algorithm that uses an LLM and a formal solver to sample proofs as observations of individuals' distinct commonsense beliefs, and aggregates conclusions across these samples. Empirically, PACS outperforms chain-of-thought reasoning, prior neurosymbolic methods, and search-based approaches across multiple benchmarks.

</details>


### [261] [Learning CLI Agents with Structured Action Credit under Selective Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08013)
*Haoyang Su,Ying Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出σ-Reveal（选择性观察机制）和A³（行动优势分配方法）来解决CLI智能体在代码库中识别任务相关证据和稀疏终端奖励分配两大瓶颈，并构建了ShellOps数据集进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CLI智能体方法未能充分利用CLI动作的原生结构化属性作为学习信号，同时面临两个关键瓶颈：1) 智能体必须从部分观测中在大规模代码库中识别任务相关证据；2) 稀疏的终端奖励必须分配给塑造长多轮轨迹的动作。

Method: 提出σ-Reveal（推理时机制，为同一CLI选择符元预算的上下文）和A³（Action Advantage Assignment，一种原生智能体RL方法，从回合级相对反馈、基于AST的动作子链残差和树级轨迹边际构建回合级优势）。

Result: 构建了ShellOps数据集（覆盖仓库环境中CLI任务的可验证数据集），并验证了所提方法在shell驱动的信息提取和文件编辑任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: σ-Reveal和A³分别针对CLI智能体的选择性观测和信用分配问题提供了有效解决方案，推动了智能体-计算机交互的实用范式发展。

Abstract: Command line interface (CLI) agents are emerging as a practical paradigm for agent-computer interaction over evolving filesystems, executable command line programs, and online execution feedback. Recent work has used reinforcement learning (RL) to learn these interaction abilities from verifiable task feedback, yet few methods exploit the native structured attributes of CLI actions as learning signals. Beyond this underused action structure, CLI learning also couples two bottlenecks for coding agents. First, the agent must identify task-relevant evidence in a large codebase from partial observations. Second, sparse terminal rewards must be assigned to the actions that shape a long multi-turn trajectory. We study these bottlenecks through shell-driven information extraction and file editing tasks. For selective observation, we introduce $σ$-Reveal, an inference-time mechanism that selects token-budgeted context for the same CLI. For credit assignment, we propose Action Advantage Assignment ($\mathrm{A}^3$), a native agentic RL method that preserves the algorithmic complexity of standard agentic RL. $\mathrm{A}^3$ constructs turn-level advantages from episode-level relative feedback, abstract syntax tree (AST) based action sub-chain residuals, and tree-level trajectory margins. To further evaluate this problem setting, we construct ShellOps, a verifiable dataset suite covering CLI tasks in repository environments.

</details>


### [262] [VecCISC: Improving Confidence-Informed Self-Consistency with Reasoning Trace Clustering and Candidate Answer Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08070)
*James Petullo,Sonny George,Dylan Cashman,Nianwen Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出VecCISC框架，通过语义相似性过滤冗余推理轨迹，在保持或提升加权多数投票精度的同时，减少47%的token使用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加权多数投票（如CISC）虽比标准Self-Consistency更准确，但需要额外的critic LLM为每个候选答案生成置信度分数，导致高昂的计算开销和成本。为降低这一开销，需要一种轻量级方法减少需要critic评估的候选答案数量。

Method: VecCISC利用语义相似性度量，过滤掉语义等价、退化或幻觉的推理轨迹，从而减少需要输入给critic LLM进行置信度评估的候选答案数量。该框架是轻量级且自适应的。

Result: 在数学、化学、生物学、常识推理和人文学科五个领域的基准测试上，VecCISC将总token使用量减少47%，同时保持或超过了CISC的准确率。

Conclusion: VecCISC通过语义过滤有效降低了加权多数投票的计算成本，在不牺牲性能的前提下实现了显著的效率提升，为推理时扩展技术提供了更具实用性的方案。

Abstract: A standard technique for scaling inference-time reasoning is Self-Consistency, whereby multiple candidate answers are sampled from an LLM and the most common answer is selected. More recently, it has been shown that weighted majority voting (e.g. Confidence-Informed Self Consistency (CISC)), which assigns a confidence value to each candidate answer and chooses the answer with the largest accumulated score, tends to be more accurate on a wide range of popular benchmarks. In practice, weighted majority voting necessitates calling a critic LLM on each candidate's reasoning trace to produce the answer's confidence score. This secondary series of LLM calls greatly increases the overhead and cost of weighted majority voting, despite its potential performance benefits. To reduce this expense, we propose VecCISC, a lightweight, adaptive framework that uses a measure of semantic similarity to filter reasoning traces that are semantically equivalent to others, degenerate, or hallucinated, thus decreasing the number of candidate answers that must be evaluated by the critic. To ensure adequate experimental thoroughness, we evaluate VecCISC on five challenging, widely-adopted datasets spanning the domains of mathematics, chemistry, biology, commonsense reasoning, and the humanities. Our results demonstrate that VecCISC reduces the total token usage by 47%, while maintaining or exceeding the accuracy of CISC.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [263] [Modular Lie Algebraic PDE Control of Multibody Flexible Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06709)
*Sadeq Yaqubi,Jouni Mattila*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对柔性多体机器人系统，提出了一种基于PDE控制的子系统框架，利用螺旋理论和李代数模型，通过应变变形PDE消除分布式弹性加速度，结合自适应控制和Lyapunov稳定性分析，实现了任意长度串联机械臂的全局指数跟踪误差收敛和有界弹性变形保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的柔性机器人控制方法在处理多连杆、大范围3D空间运动时，面临模型复杂、子系统的耦合交互难以精确处理的挑战，缺乏模块化且可扩展的稳定性保证框架。

Method: 采用螺旋理论李代数模型，将运动、变形和力统一表示为se(3)中的体固定旋量和力旋量；通过应变变形PDE替代弹性动力学，消除分布式弹性加速度；使用偏转补偿逆运动学从任务空间端点生成子系统轨迹；设计名义控制器实现误差指数衰减，并结合自适应参数估计；利用功率配对在se(3)×se*(3)上的框架不变性和牛顿第三定律，使连杆间相互作用功率项在复合Lyapunov函数求导时自洽抵消。

Result: 建立了全局指数跟踪误差收敛的稳定性证明；通过Euler-Bernoulli能量论证保证了有界弹性变形；螺旋理论结构使得交互抵消精确成立，稳定性证书模块化且可扩展到任意长度连杆链；数值仿真验证了方案的物理一致性。

Conclusion: 所提框架为柔性多体机器人控制提供了一种可扩展、模块化的稳定方案，螺旋理论的结构特性使得子系统间的耦合交互在Lyapunov分析中自然抵消，为更复杂柔性机器人系统的分布式控制奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: This paper addresses PDE-based control for flexible multibody robotic systems, presenting a subsystem-based framework for serial manipulators with arbitrary links in 3D space. The approach uses a screw-theoretic Lie-algebraic model where motion, deformation, and forces are expressed as body-fixed twists and wrenches in se(3). By substituting a strain-based deformation PDE into the dynamics, distributed elastic acceleration is eliminated, yielding a model governed by twist acceleration and the deformation field. Subsystem twist trajectories are generated from task-space endpoints via deflection-compensating inverse kinematics, providing real-time correction for tip deformation. A nominal controller for each link ensures exponential decay of twist errors via a Lyapunov function nu_i. An adaptive modification replaces physical parameters with online estimates, establishing exponential convergence of both twist and parameter errors. Summing over all links, composite Lyapunov functions V = sum(nu_i) and V^a = sum(nu_i^a) yield time derivatives where inter-link interaction power terms telescope to zero. This cancellation is ensured by Newton's third law and the frame invariance of the power pairing on se(3) x se*(3), establishing global exponential convergence of tracking errors. Bounded elastic deformation is guaranteed by an Euler-Bernoulli energy argument. The screw-theoretic structure renders interaction cancellation exact, making the stability certificate modular and scalable to chains of arbitrary length. Numerical simulations demonstrate the scheme's physical consistency.

</details>


### [264] [An Aerial Manipulator for Perception-Driven Flower Targeting Toward Contactless Pollination in Vertical Farming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06759)
*Chenzhe Jin,Zhuohang Wu,Yifan Cai,Xiangqi Li,Jan Ming Kevin Tan,Narsimlu Kemsaram,Valerio Modugno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于垂直农业环境中的空中操作平台，集成了RGBD感知、MPPI无人机控制和2自由度机械臂，实现了厘米级精度的花朵定位和末端执行器对准，为未来非接触式授粉系统奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然传粉者减少对受控室内农业（尤其是垂直农场）的作物生产构成重大挑战，亟需开发能够精准进行花朵定位且最小化对脆弱花结构物理干扰的机器人系统。

Method: 搭建了集成RGBD感知、基于模型预测路径积分（MPPI）的PX4无人机控制以及轻量级2自由度机械臂的空中操作平台，在MuJoCo仿真和真实UAV实验室环境中进行了花朵定位测试验证。

Result: 仿真实验中控制器实现了一致的轨迹收敛和精确目标对准；真实实验中实现了稳定无人机飞行、可靠花朵定位和厘米级末端执行器定位精度。

Conclusion: 验证了所提出的空中操作平台作为未来非接触式授粉系统的鲁棒机器人载体和定位框架的有效性，为集成声学花粉操控等先进非接触式末端执行器提供了基础。

Abstract: The decline of natural pollinators has created a major challenge for crop production in controlled indoor agriculture, particularly in vertical farming environments where natural insect pollination is absent. This motivates the development of robotic systems capable of performing precise flower targeting tasks while minimizing physical interference with delicate floral structures. This paper presents an aerial manipulator platform for perception driven flower detection, localization, and approach in vertical farming environments. The proposed system integrates onboard RGBD based perception, model predictive path integral (MPPI) based unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) control on a PX4 platform, and a lightweight 2DoF manipulator for precise end effector positioning. The platform is evaluated in both MuJoCo simulation and UAV lab experiments using a flower targeting testbed. The experimental results demonstrate stable UAV flight, reliable flower localization, and centimeter level end effector positioning accuracy. In simulation, the proposed controller achieves consistent trajectory convergence and accurate target alignment. In the real world UAV lab environment, the integrated perception control manipulation framework enables stable flower targeted positioning and end effector alignment under constrained aerial operation. These results validate the proposed aerial manipulator as a robust robotic carrier and positioning framework for future contactless pollination systems. While the current study focuses on perception guided targeting and positioning, the developed platform provides a practical foundation for integrating advanced contactless end effectors, including acoustic based pollen manipulation modules, in future work.

</details>


### [265] [Bi3: A Biplatform, Bicultural, Biperson Dataset for Social Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06863)
*Andrew Stratton,Phani Teja Singamaneni,Pranav Goyal,Rachid Alami,Christoforos Mavrogiannis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Bi3是一个在受限实验室空间中人机群组导航数据集，包含74名参与者、5种导航算法、2种机器人平台，以及10.5小时的多模态数据（运动轨迹、RGB视频、用户印象），具有独特的多样性和建模复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社交机器人导航数据集缺乏在受限空间中的人机密集交互场景，难以支撑复杂环境下的人机协同导航研究。

Method: 设计原始实验，让两名人类与机器人在受限空间内产生近距离导航遭遇；收集包含5种导航算法、2种机器人平台的数据；从美国和法国两个站点招募74名参与者；采集运动轨迹、RGB视频和用户主观评价等多模态数据。

Result: 通过交互密度和人类速度等指标分析，表明Bi3数据集在多样性和建模复杂度上具有基准性。

Conclusion: Bi3数据集有助于理解受限环境中人机活动的有效融合，可作为人类运动预测模型和拥挤空间导航机器人控制策略的训练资源。

Abstract: We contribute Bi3, a dataset of social robot navigation among groups of people in a constrained lab space. Compared to prior data collection efforts for social robot navigation, our dataset is unique in that it features: an original experiment design giving rise to close navigation encounters between two humans and a robot; five different navigation algorithms; two different robot platforms; a diverse participant pool of 74 people recruited from two sites in the USA and France; multimodal data streams including 10.5 hours of human and robot ground-truth motion tracks, RGB video, and user impressions over robot performance. Our analysis of the collected dataset through metrics like interaction density and human velocity suggests that Bi3 represents a benchmark of unique diversity and modeling complexity. Bi3 contributes towards understanding how humans and robots can productively mesh their activities in constrained environments, and can be a resource for training models of human motion prediction and robot control policies for navigation in densely crowded spaces.

</details>


### [266] [Traffic Scenario Orchestration from Language via Constraint Satisfaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06966)
*Frieda Rong,Chris Zhang,Kelvin Wong,Raquel Urtasun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将自然语言场景描述转化为约束求解问题的方法，利用基础模型推理生成约束，再通过求解器生成符合测试意图的自动驾驶闭环测试场景，在基准测试中显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需要在仿真中进行大量测试，但测试场景生成既费力又难以兼顾可控性和规模。手动编程可控性强但难以扩展，统计模型虽可扩展却难以实现精确可控，特别是在分布外场景中。

Method: 将场景编排建模为约束求解问题，采用"语言输入-仿真输出"的架构：首先利用基础模型推理将自然语言描述转化为一组约束作为场景表示，然后使用现成的求解器求解满足闭环测试意图的智能体行为。

Result: 在精心设计的多样化场景描述基准上，该方法在编排成功率上大幅超越基线方法。特别地，闭环方法对于需要自我反应性（ego-reactive）规范的场景尤为重要。

Conclusion: 将自然语言与约束求解相结合的方法能够兼顾场景编排的可控性与可扩展性，基础模型的推理能力与求解器的精确性互补，为自动驾驶闭环测试提供了一种高效的场景生成方案。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles (AVs) require extensive testing in simulation, but test case generation for driving scenarios is laborious. The desired scenarios are often out-of-distribution and have precise requirements on interactions with the AV policy under test. Manually programming scenarios allows for precise controllability but is difficult to scale. On the other hand, statistical models can leverage compute and data, but struggle with precise controllability when out-of-distribution. We cast scenario orchestration as a constraint-solving problem and present a language-in, simulation-out scenario orchestrator for closed-loop testing AVs. Our approach leverages foundation model reasoning to translate general, natural language descriptions into a set of constraints as a scenario representation. This then allows us to leverage off the shelf solvers to solve for actor behaviors which meet precise testing intentions in closed-loop. Under a benchmark of carefully crafted and diverse scenario descriptions, our approach greatly outperforms our baselines in orchestration success rate. We further show that our closed-loop approach is especially important for scenarios which require ego-reactive specifications.

</details>


### [267] [AirBender: Adaptive Transportation of Bendable Objects Using Dual UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07003)
*Jiawei Xu,Longsen Gao,Rafael Fierro,David Saldaña*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应控制器，使两架飞行器无需显式弹性模型即可协作运输可弯曲物体，并通过李雅普诺夫分析证明其渐近稳定性，硬件实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机器人与可弯曲物体交互时控制困难，由于驱动能力有限且需持续悬停，性能易下降甚至坠毁，现有方法依赖显式弹性模型，适应性不足。

Method: 提出自适应控制器，允许两架飞行器协作跟踪轨迹并运输可弯曲物体，无需显式弹性模型；可在飞行中自适应物体未知变形特性，并采用李雅普诺夫分析证明渐近稳定性。

Result: 通过多种场景下的硬件实验评估，展示了多旋翼飞行器处理可弯曲物体的能力，验证了轨迹跟踪的稳定性和性能。

Conclusion: 所提自适应控制器有效解决了空中机器人运输可弯曲物体的控制难题，无需先验弹性模型即可保证稳定性和轨迹跟踪性能。

Abstract: The interaction of robots with bendable objects in midair presents significant challenges in control, often resulting in performance degradation and potential crashes, especially for aerial robots due to their limited actuation capabilities and constant need to remain airborne. This paper presents an adaptive controller that enables two aerial vehicles to collaboratively follow a trajectory while transporting a bendable object without relying on explicit elasticity models. Our method allows on-the-fly adaptation to the object's unknown deformable properties, ensuring stability and performance in trajectory-tracking tasks. We use Lyapunov analysis to demonstrate that our adaptive controller is asymptotically stable. Our method is evaluated through hardware experiments in various scenarios, demonstrating the capabilities of using multirotor aerial vehicles to handle bendable objects.

</details>


### [268] [Intention assimilation control for accurate tracking with variable impedance in teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07037)
*Atsushi Takagi,Yanan Li,Hiroaki Gomi,Etienne Burdet*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出意图同化控制（IAC）策略，通过估计领导者目标位置而非当前位置，在低刚度下实现高跟踪精度，解决了跟踪精度与安全性之间的权衡问题，在7自由度机械臂上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统遥操作中弹簧类力控制策略存在跟踪精度与安全性的固有矛盾：低刚度跟踪精度差，高刚度对环境和人构成危险。需要一种能同时保证跟踪精度和操作安全性的控制方法。

Method: 提出意图同化控制（IAC）策略，不再传输领导者当前位置，而是估计领导者目标位置并传输给跟随者。跟随者阻抗可动态调整，以反映用户期望阻抗或自动满足任务需求。

Result: 在7自由度机械臂上经四项实验（自由跟踪、气球交互、插销插入、力反馈抛光）验证，IAC相比遥阻抗控制（TIC）提高了跟踪精度、任务完成率，减少了完成时间。

Conclusion: IAC使机器人能在低刚度下精确复制用户运动，同时允许用户根据意图自由调节阻抗，在单边和双边遥操作中实现了对跟随者位置和阻抗前所未有的控制水平。

Abstract: Robot systems for teleoperation commonly use a spring-like force pulling the follower robot towards the leader's position to track their movements. With this control strategy, the tracking accuracy deteriorates when the follower' stiffness is low, but high stiffness poses a danger to objects or people in the follower robot's environment. To address this trade-off between tracking accuracy and safety, we propose an alternative intention assimilation control (IAC) strategy where the robot's tracking accuracy can be ensured without high stiffness. Different from traditional approaches, which transmit the leader's current position to the follower, this new controller estimates the leader's target position and transmits it to the follower. With this strategy, the follower impedance can be changed on-the-fly to continuously reflect the user's desired impedance or modulated automatically to fulfill the task requirements. Our controller was validated on two 7 degree-of-freedom manipulators, yielding high tracking accuracy with varying impedance. Four experiments were conducted to compare {teleoperation} with IAC to tele-impedance control (TIC) during free tracking, interaction with a balloon, during peg insertion, and table polishing with force feedback. The results show that IAC increases tracking accuracy, improves task completion rate and reduces completion time. IAC enables the robot to accurately replicate the user's movement while giving them freedom to modulate the impedance according to their intention, providing an unprecedented level of control of the follower's position and its impedance during unilateral and bilateral teleoperation.

</details>


### [269] [Dr-BA: Separable Optimization for Direct Radar Bundle Adjustment & Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07041)
*Daniil Lisus,Cedric Le Gentil,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Dr-BA是首个直接作用于2D旋转雷达强度图像的雷达束调整框架，通过可分离优化解耦位姿估计与建图，实现密集地图构建与传感器位姿联合估计，在超过200公里道路数据上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于旋转雷达的状态估计方法通常从距离-方位-强度测量中提取稀疏点云并进行点云配准，未能充分利用雷达完整回波信息。雷达在降水等恶劣天气下不受影响，是实现全天气鲁棒自动驾驶的关键传感器，亟需一种能直接利用完整雷达强度图像进行联合优化估计的方法。

Method: 将束调整问题建模为可分离优化，解耦位姿估计与建图过程，直接在2D旋转雷达强度图像上操作，利用多个扫描的完整雷达回波联合估计密集地图和传感器位姿，并自然扩展至仅使用雷达的在先前建图中定位（DRL）。

Result: 在五条不同路线超过200公里的道路数据上验证，Dr-BA在基于雷达的束调整和跨会话定位任务中达到当前最优性能。

Conclusion: Dr-BA证明了直接利用完整雷达强度图像进行束调整的可行性，为全天气自动驾驶中的雷达状态估计提供了新的有效范式，且代码已开源。

Abstract: This paper introduces Dr-BA, a first-of-its-kind radar bundle adjustment (BA) framework that operates directly on 2D spinning radar intensity images. Unlike camera or lidar sensors, radar is largely unaffected by precipitation, making it a critical modality for autonomous systems that require all-weather robustness. Existing state estimation approaches using spinning radar typically extract sparse point clouds from range-azimuth-intensity measurements and apply point cloud alignment techniques to estimate vehicle motion, scene structure, or to localize within an existing map. In contrast, Dr-BA uses the full radar returns from multiple scans to jointly estimate dense maps and sensor poses. By formulating the problem as a separable optimization, we derive an efficient and general solution that decouples pose estimation from mapping. In addition to solving the BA problem, this formulation naturally extends to direct radar-only localization (DRL) within a previously built map. Dr-BA achieves state-of-the-art radar-based BA and cross-session localization performance, demonstrated on more than 200 km of on-road data across five distinct routes. Our implementation is publicly available at https://github.com/utiasASRL/dr_ba.

</details>


### [270] [PISTO: Proximal Inference for Stochastic Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07215)
*Hongzhe Yu,Zinuo Chang,Yongxin Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PISTO算法，通过将STOMP的KL散度最小化解释与近端KL正则化结合，实现了更稳定的随机轨迹优化，在机器人规划与运动任务中优于CHOMP、STOMP、CEM和MPPI等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STOMP虽然能处理不可微成本函数，但其更新机制缺乏清晰的概率解释且稳定性不足。作者旨在揭示STOMP的变分推断结构，并在此基础上提出更稳定的优化算法。

Method: 将STOMP的更新解释为最小化玻尔兹曼轨迹分布的KL散度，进而提出PISTO算法。PISTO在目标函数中引入连续高斯提议分布之间的KL正则化项（近端项），形成信任域约束，得到闭式均值更新解；通过重要性加权蒙特卡洛采样估计期望值，无需梯度信息。

Result: 在机械臂运动规划中，PISTO成功率达89%，优于CHOMP（63%）和STOMP（68%），路径更短更平滑，速度是其他随机方法的2倍。在接触丰富的MuJoCo locomotion和manipulation任务中，PISTO在奖励上持续优于CEM和MPPI基线。

Conclusion: PISTO通过近端KL正则化稳定了随机轨迹优化过程，在保持处理不可微/不连续成本能力的同时，显著提升了规划成功率和效率，为基于采样的轨迹优化提供了新的理论基础和改进方向。

Abstract: Stochastic trajectory optimization methods like STOMP enable planning with non-differentiable costs, offering substantial flexibility over gradient-based approaches. We show that STOMP implicitly minimizes the KL divergence from a Boltzmann trajectory distribution, revealing an elegant Variational Inference (VI) structure underlying its updates. Building on this insight, we propose the \textit{Proximal Inference for Stochastic Trajectory Optimization} (PISTO) algorithm that stabilizes the updates by augmenting the objective with a KL regularization between successive Gaussian proposals. This proximal formulation admits a trust-region interpretation and yields closed-form mean updates computable as expectations under a surrogate distribution. We estimate these expectations via importance-weighted Monte Carlo sampling, producing a simple, derivative-free algorithm that inherits STOMP's ability to handle non-differentiable and discontinuous costs without modification. On robot arm motion planning benchmarks, PISTO achieves an 89\% success rate -- outperforming CHOMP (63\%) and STOMP (68\%) -- while producing shorter, smoother paths at twice the speed of competing stochastic methods. We further validate PISTO on contact-rich MuJoCo locomotion and manipulation tasks, where it consistently outperforms both CEM and MPPI baselines in reward.

</details>


### [271] [Palm-sized Omnidirectional Vision-Based UAV Exploration with Sparse Topological Map Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07275)
*Zirui Wang,Xinjia Luo,Haotian Sun,Jun Ma,Jian Guo,Boyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级自主探索系统，利用全向视觉和稀疏拓扑地图引导，在满足SWaP约束的微型无人机上实现低计算消耗的高效探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统探索方法依赖密集占用地图或高分辨率点云，导致内存和计算开销大；且微型无人机受尺寸、重量和功率（SWaP）限制，难以搭载激光雷达等传感器获取精确几何测量。

Method: 采用多鱼眼相机实现全向视场和深度估计；将前沿表示为拓扑节点的潜在未探索区域，而非显式边界；使用由关键节点及其描述符组成的稀疏拓扑地图替代传统密集表示；直接在稀疏图上进行全局路径规划。

Result: 在仿真和实际实验中，在轴距11厘米、重400克的掌上视觉无人机上验证了该方法能够以极低计算消耗实现高效探索。

Conclusion: 该方法通过全向视觉与稀疏拓扑地图的结合，有效降低了内存和计算需求，使SWaP受限的微型无人机也能实现自主探索。

Abstract: Classic exploration methods often rely on dense occupancy maps or high-resolution point clouds for frontier detection and path planning, resulting in substantial memory consumption and computational overhead. Moreover, micro UAVs under size, weight, and power (SWaP) constraints are not practical to be equipped with sensors like LiDAR to obtain accurate environmental geometric measurements. This paper presents a lightweight autonomous exploration system that leverages omnidirectional vision and sparse topological map guidance. Specifically, we utilize a multi-fisheye camera setup to achieve omnidirectional Field of View (FoV) and perform depth estimation. To address the limited depth estimation accuracy, frontiers are represented as potential unexplored regions characterized by topological nodes instead of explicit boundaries, enabling efficient identification of frontier regions without maintaining occupancy grids or global point clouds. Unlike classic dense representations, our approach abstracts the environment using a sparse topological map composed of key nodes and their descriptors, reducing memory consumption and computational demands. Global path planning is performed directly on the sparse graph. The proposed method is validated in both simulation and on a palm-sized vision-based UAV with an 11 cm wheelbase and a 400 g weight in real-world experiments, demonstrating that our method can achieve efficient exploration with extremely low computational consumption.

</details>


### [272] [Variable Aerodynamic Damping via Co-Contraction: A Dynamic Isomorphism with Variable Stiffness Actuators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07292)
*Antonio Franchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文证明冗余双旋翼执行器中的气动共收缩可以通过调整被动、配平定义的空气机械阻尼，同时保持指令净力恒定。提出了增量阻尼系数概念并证明其在恒定力纤维上单调递增，基于叶素理论推导了最小推力模型，形式化为可变气动阻尼执行器（VADA），并证明其在动力学上与拮抗式变刚度执行器（VSA）中的刚度调制同构。该机制还可增强冗余多旋翼的主动气动快速性度量，最后通过阻抗形式表示阐明了共模与差模驱动在被动阻抗和平衡速度控制中的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在冗余双旋翼系统中通过气动共收缩实现阻尼调制的机制，以在不改变净推力的情况下调控被动阻抗特性，从而提高系统的控制灵活性。

Method: 定义了增量阻尼系数作为净推力对气动相对速度在配平处的局部灵敏度；通过叶素理论推导了蕴含速度-入流耦合的最小推力模型；基于纤维密度原理形式化VADA执行器；并与拮抗式VSA进行动力学同构分析。

Result: 在气动硬化条件下，证明了增量阻尼系数沿恒定力纤维单调递增；验证了阻尼和硬化特性；建立了VADA与VSA之间的动力学同构关系；发现该原理也能增强冗余多旋翼的主动气动快速性。

Conclusion: 气动共收缩是一种有效的被动阻抗调节机制，可与肌肉共收缩类比，为多旋翼系统的阻抗控制提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We prove that aerodynamic co-contraction in a redundant dual-rotor actuator can tune a passive, trim-defined aero-mechanical damping while keeping the commanded net force constant. In particular, we define an incremental damping coefficient as the local sensitivity of net thrust to air-relative velocity at a trim and prove that it increases monotonically along constant-force fibers under a mild aerodynamic hardening condition. We then validate the required damping and hardening properties from a first-principles Blade Element Theory derivation, which yields a minimal thrust model affine in inflow and explicitly reveals the speed--inflow coupling driving the effect. The resulting mechanism is formalized as a Variable Aerodynamic Damping Actuator (VADA) and shown to be dynamically isomorphic to stiffness modulation in antagonistic variable-stiffness actuation (VSA), similar to the co-contraction of tendons by muscle co-activation. The same fiber-density principle also enhances the active aerodynamic promptness measure of redundant multirotors. Finally, an impedance-form representation clarifies the roles of common-mode and differential-mode actuation in the control of passive impedance and the equilibrium velocity of the VADA system.

</details>


### [273] [BioProVLA-Agent: An Affordable, Protocol-Driven, Vision-Enhanced VLA-Enabled Embodied Multi-Agent System with Closed-Loop-Capable Reasoning for Biological Laboratory Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07306)
*Zhaohui Du,Zhe Wang,Hongmei Fei,Xiwen Cao,Ting Xiao,Qi Wang,Huanbo Jin,Jiaming Gu,Quan Lu,Zhe Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出BioProVLA-Agent，一个基于视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型、以实验协议为接口、结合协议解析、视觉状态验证和实体执行的闭环多智能体系统，用于生物实验室自动化操作。通过AugSmolVLA在线增强策略应对透明器皿、反射等视觉扰动，在15个原子任务、6个复合工作流和3个双手任务上验证了系统的鲁棒性和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物实验室自动化可减少重复性人工操作并提高可重复性，但湿实验室环境中可靠的实体执行仍面临挑战：协议结构不统一、器皿透明或反光、多步骤流程需要状态感知执行，而现有机器人系统依赖昂贵硬件、固定工作流或专用设备。

Method: BioProVLA-Agent采用多智能体系统架构，包含三个核心组件：1) Tailored LLM Protocol Agent将自然语言协议转化为可验证的子任务；2) VLM-RAG Verification Agent利用观测、机器人状态、检索知识和成功/失败示例评估任务准备与完成状态；3) VLA Embodied Agent通过轻量策略执行已验证的子任务。此外提出AugSmolVLA在线增强策略，针对透明器皿、反射、光照变化和过曝等湿实验室视觉扰动进行鲁棒性优化。

Result: 在包含15个原子任务、6个复合工作流和3个双手任务的分层基准上评估，包括试管装载、分拣、废料处理、旋盖和液体倾倒等。在正常和高曝光条件下，AugSmolVLA在精确放置、透明物体操作、复合工作流和视觉退化场景下的执行稳定性均优于ACT、X-VLA和原始SmolVLA。

Conclusion: 系统展示了一条面向生物操作的、可获取的、以协议为中心且具备验证能力的具身AI实用路径，为生物实验室自动化提供了低成本、协议驱动的解决方案。

Abstract: Biological laboratory automation can reduce repetitive manual work and improve reproducibility, but reliable embodied execution in wet-lab environments remains challenging. Protocols are often unstructured, labware is frequently transparent or reflective, and multi-step procedures require state-aware execution beyond one-shot instruction following. Existing robotic systems often rely on costly hardware, fixed workflows, dedicated instruments, or robotics-oriented interfaces. Here, we introduce BioProVLA-Agent, an affordable, protocol-driven, vision-enhanced embodied multi-agent system enabled by Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for biological manipulation. The system uses protocols as the task interface and integrates protocol parsing, visual state verification, and embodied execution in a closed-loop workflow. A Tailored LLM Protocol Agent converts protocols into verifiable subtasks; a VLM-RAG Verification Agent assesses readiness and completion using observations, robot states, retrieved knowledge, and success/failure examples; and a VLA Embodied Agent executes verified subtasks through a lightweight policy. To improve robustness under wet-lab visual perturbations, we develop AugSmolVLA, an online augmentation strategy targeting transparent labware, reflections, illumination shifts, and overexposure. We evaluate the system on a hierarchical benchmark covering 15 atomic tasks, 6 composite workflows, and 3 bimanual tasks, including tube loading, sorting, waste disposal, cap twisting, and liquid pouring. Across normal and high-exposure settings, AugSmolVLA improves execution stability over ACT, X-VLA, and the original SmolVLA, especially for precise placement, transparent-object manipulation, composite workflows, and visually degraded scenes. These results suggest a practical route toward accessible, protocol-centered, and verification-capable embodied AI for biological manipulation.

</details>


### [274] [AT-VLA: Adaptive Tactile Injection for Enhanced Feedback Reaction in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07308)
*Xiaoqi Li,Muhe Cai,Jiadong Xu,Juan Zhu,Hongwei Fan,Yan Shen,Guangrui Ren,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AT-VLA模型，通过自适应触觉注入机制和触觉反应双流机制，在不破坏预训练能力的前提下实现快速触觉反馈，解决VLA在接触密集型操作中精度不足和推理速度慢的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在接触密集型操作场景中面临精确物理交互的挑战，引入触觉信号进行微调会破坏预训练能力，且VLA推理速度慢无法实现实时触觉反馈响应。

Method: 提出自适应触觉注入机制（动态决定触觉注入的时机和位置，仅在显著贡献于动作生成时注入）和触觉反应双流机制（将感知处理解耦为低频视觉-语言流用于感知推理，高频触觉控制流用于物理交互理解，实现0.04秒内实时闭环响应）。

Result: 在真实世界的接触密集型操作任务中验证了AT-VLA的有效性。

Conclusion: AT-VLA成功解决了VLA在接触密集型操作中触觉信号引入导致的预训练能力干扰问题，并通过双流机制实现了快速准确的触觉响应。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have significantly advanced the capabilities of robotic agents in executing diverse tasks; however, they still face challenges in contact-rich manipulation scenarios that require precise physical interactions. To address this limitation, recent studies have attempted to incorporate tactile signals during downstream tasks, enabling pretrained VLAs to interpret tactile feedback. Nevertheless, introducing new modalities during finetuning, which are rarely present in the pretrain stage, may disrupt the pretrained capabilities of VLAs. In addition, the inherently slow inference speed of VLAs hampers real-time responsiveness and limits the effective utilization of tactile feedback for action adjustment. To overcome these challenges, we propose Adaptive Tactile Vision-Language-Action (AT-VLA), which introduces a novel Adaptive Tactile Injection mechanism. This mechanism dynamically determines the appropriate timing and locations for tactile injection, incorporating only when it significantly contributes to action generation, thereby minimizing interference with pretrained representations. Furthermore, to enable rapid and accurate tactile responses, we propose a Tactile Reaction Dual-Stream mechanism, which decouples sensory processing into a slow visual-language stream for low-frequency perceptual reasoning and a fast tactile control stream for high-frequency physical interaction understanding, achieving real-time close-loop responses within 0.04 s. Real-world experiments thoroughly validate the effectiveness of AT-VLA in contact-rich manipulation tasks. The project page is available at: https://sites.google.com/view/at-vla.

</details>


### [275] [CSR: Infinite-Horizon Real-Time Policies with Massive Cached State Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07325)
*Robin Karlsson,Go Suzui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CSR框架和ASR算法，通过优化KV缓存重用和异步状态内存回收，将大规模LLM部署为机器人持续认知引擎的TTFT延迟从14.67s降至0.56s（26倍提升），实现高频（>2Hz）具身策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模LLM作为机器人连续认知引擎时，处理长历史状态信息面临TTFT延迟瓶颈，现有方案（RAG、滑动窗口）无法同时兼顾全局上下文和低延迟。

Method: 首先形式化最优任务结构并理论证明前缀稳定性、增量可扩展性和异步状态协调是实时性能的必要条件。基于此设计CSR框架实现最优KV缓存重用，并提出ASR算法将状态内存驱逐卸载到并行计算资源以消除延迟尖峰。

Result: 在物理机器人上，120K token上下文、235B模型实现26倍延迟降低（14.67s→0.56s）；在具身AI基准上达到SOTA召回率（0.836 vs 0.459）并保持RAG级别延迟；ASR在10次连续驱逐周期中维持有界无尖峰TTFT。

Conclusion: CSR与ASR相结合使大规模LLM能够作为连续运行的高频（>2Hz）具身策略，突破了LLM用于实时机器人控制的延迟瓶颈。

Abstract: Deploying massive large language models (LLMs) as continuous cognitive engines for robotics is bottlenecked by the time-to-first-token (TTFT) latency required to process extensive state histories. Existing solutions like RAG or sliding windows compromise global context or incur prohibitive re-computation costs. We formalize the optimal task structure for minimizing latency and theoretically prove that prefix stability, incremental extensibility, and asynchronous state reconciliation are necessary conditions for real-time performance. Building on these proofs, we introduce the Cached State Representation (CSR) framework as the practical instantiation of these properties, ensuring optimal KV-cache reuse. To sustain these properties over infinite horizons, we further propose an Asynchronous State Reconciliation (ASR) algorithm that offloads state memory eviction to a parallel computational resource to eliminate latency spikes. On a physical robot wirelessly connected to an on-premise GPU server, CSR achieves a 26-fold latency reduction (14.67s to 0.56s) for 120K token contexts with a 235B parameter model compared to a standard baseline. On an embodied AI benchmark, we achieve SOTA recall (0.836 vs. 0.459) while maintaining RAG-level latency. ASR is validated to sustain bounded, spike-free TTFT over 10 eviction cycles in continuous real-world operation. Together, CSR and ASR enable massive LLMs to function as continuously operating, high-frequency (> 2 Hz) embodied policies.

</details>


### [276] [Weather-Robust Scene Semantics with Vision-Aligned 4D Radar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07367)
*Kali Hamilton,Christoffer Heckman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将雷达编码器与冻结的SigLIP视觉嵌入对齐的方法，通过约7M可训练参数解码结构化场景描述，在雨雾雪等恶劣天气下性能优于摄像头基线，并发现令牌-范数不匹配是主要失败模式，通过投影层输出层归一化可解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摄像头和LiDAR在雨、雾、雪等恶劣天气下性能严重下降，而毫米波雷达几乎不受影响，因此需要探索如何利用雷达数据进行可靠的场景理解。

Method: 将雷达编码器与冻结的SigLIP视觉嵌入对齐，通过大约7M可训练参数，利用冻结的视觉-语言模型解码结构化场景描述。

Result: 在K-RADAR数据集的留出雾、小雪、大雪序列上，所有雷达配置均优于摄像头基线（摄像头基线产生超过90%的幻觉）。

Conclusion: 令牌-范数不匹配是桥接雷达与冻结VLM时的主要失败模式，投影层输出层归一化可有效解决；编码器复杂度、字幕格式和池化策略的选择存在权衡，为未来雷达-VLM流水线设计提供指导。

Abstract: Cameras and LiDAR degrade in rain, fog, and snow, while millimeter-wave radar remains largely unaffected. We align a radar encoder to frozen SigLIP vision
  embeddings and decode structured scene captions through a frozen vision-language model (VLM) with approximately 7M trainable parameters. On K-RADAR with
  held-out fog, light snow, and heavy snow sequences, all radar configurations outperform a camera baseline that collapses to over 90% hallucination. We
  identify a token-norm mismatch as the dominant failure mode when bridging radar to a frozen VLM and show that projector-output LayerNorm resolves it.
  Analysis of encoder complexity, caption format, and pooling strategy reveals tradeoffs that inform future radar-VLM pipeline design.

</details>


### [277] [MORPH-U: Multi-Objective Resilient Motion Planning for V2X-Enabled Autonomous Driving in High-Uncertainty Environments via Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07370)
*Shih-Yu Lai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MORPH-U框架，通过融合V2X通信与多传感器构建局部动态地图(LDM)，结合混合A*重规划和拜占庭式攻击防御门控机制，实现不确定事件驱动条件下自动驾驶的鲁棒运动规划与控制，并在CARLA仿真中验证了安全性、可控性权衡及抗攻击能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: V2X通信虽能提供超视距感知，但存在消息延迟、丢失甚至伪造的不确定性；同时地图知识在行驶中可能动态变化，车辆需在严苛实时性约束下重规划。现有方法缺乏对这些事件驱动不确定性的联合鲁棒处理机制。

Method: 构建MORPH-U闭环堆栈：(1) 融合LiDAR/雷达/摄像头与V2X消息(CAM/DENM)至局部动态地图(LDM)；(2) 当验证的 hazard 或地图变化影响规划路径时，触发混合A*重规划；(3) 多目标优化框架权衡跟踪误差、安全裕度(TTC)、响应性和平滑性，通过帕累托前沿分析选择操作点；(4) 拜占庭式接受门控，结合法定人数规则与车载传感器否决权防御虚假V2X触发。

Result: 动态CARLA场景实验表明：V2X增强的LDM提升了下游安全性；帕累托调优提供了可控的精度-舒适性权衡；在饱和虚假DENM注入攻击(p_attack=1.0)下，门控机制成功阻止了不安全重规划。

Conclusion: MORPH-U有效融合V2X与多传感器信息，通过帕累托分析实现规划控制的可调谐权衡，并采用轻量级拜占庭防御机制抵御恶意V2X攻击，为不确定事件驱动场景下的自动驾驶提供了可行的鲁棒方案。

Abstract: V2X can warn an autonomous vehicle about hazards beyond line-of-sight, but it also brings uncertainty: messages may be delayed, dropped, or even forged. Meanwhile, map knowledge may change during a trip, forcing the vehicle to replan under tight real-time budgets. This paper studies how to make motion planning and low-level control robust to such uncertain, event-driven updates. We present MORPH-U, a CARLA-based closed-loop stack that fuses LiDAR/radar/camera with V2X (CAM/DENM) into a Local Dynamic Map (LDM) and triggers Hybrid-A* replanning when validated hazards or map changes affect the planned route. We expose the planning/control trade-offs via a multi-objective formulation over tracking error, safety margin (minimum TTC), responsiveness, and smoothness, and select operating points using Pareto-frontier analysis. To avoid unsafe replanning from faulty V2X triggers, MORPH-U adds a lightweight Byzantine-inspired acceptance gate that combines a quorum rule with an on-board sensor veto. Experiments in dynamic CARLA scenarios show that V2X-augmented LDM improves downstream safety, Pareto tuning provides controllable accuracy-comfort trade-offs, and the gate prevents replanning under saturated false-DENM injection ($p_{\text{attack}}=1.0$).

</details>


### [278] [Escaping the Diversity Trap in Robotic Manipulation via Anchor-Centric Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07381)
*Yanzhe Chen,Kevin Yuchen Ma,Qi Lv,Yiqi Lin,Zechen Bai,Chen Gao,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文发现VLA模型在数据预算受限时，传统"最大化覆盖"的多样单次演示策略会因估计噪声陷入"多样性陷阱"，导致策略性能下降。为此提出Anchor-Centric Adaptation (ACA)两阶段框架：先在核心锚点重复演示稳定策略骨架，再通过教师强制错误挖掘和约束残差更新选择性扩展高风险边界覆盖。实验证明ACA在相同预算下显著优于多样采样策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人演示数据获取成本高昂，VLA模型需要在严格数据预算下完成针对特定硬件的适应。传统启发式方法追求"最大化覆盖"来弥合具身差距，但这种方式在有限数据下可能适得其反，需要重新思考数据采集策略。

Method: 首先从理论上将策略误差分解为估计密度项和外推覆盖项，刻画了固定预算下唯一条件分配的内点最优解。然后提出ACA两阶段框架：阶段一在核心锚点收集重复演示以稳定策略骨架；阶段二通过教师强制错误挖掘识别高风险边界，并采用约束残差更新选择性扩展覆盖。

Result: 真实机器人实验验证了覆盖-密度权衡框架，证明ACA在相同数据预算下相比标准多样采样策略显著提升了任务可靠性和成功率。

Conclusion: 数据预算受限时，与其追求最大覆盖的多样采样，不如先通过重复演示稳定核心策略骨架，再针对性地扩展高风险边界覆盖。这一发现挑战了机器人学习中"多样性至上"的常见假设。

Abstract: While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer broad general capabilities, deploying them on specific hardware requires real-world adaptation to bridge the embodiment gap. Since robot demonstrations are costly, this adaptation must often occur under a strict data budget. In this work, we identify a critical diversity trap: the standard heuristic of "maximizing coverage" by collecting diverse, single-shot demonstrations can be self-defeating due to non-vanishing estimation noise. We formalize this phenomenon as a Coverage--Density Trade-off. By decomposing the policy error into estimation (density) and extrapolation (coverage) terms, we characterize an interior optimal allocation of unique conditions for a fixed budget. Guided by this analysis, we propose Anchor-Centric Adaptation (ACA), a two-stage framework that first stabilizes a policy skeleton through repeated demonstrations at core anchors, then selectively expands coverage to high-risk boundaries via teacher-forced error mining and constrained residual updates. Real-robot experiments validate our trade-off framework and demonstrate that ACA significantly improves task reliability and success rates over standard diverse sampling strategies under the same budget.

</details>


### [279] [PathPainter: Transferring the Generalization Ability of Image Generation Models to Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07496)
*Yijin Wang,Yuru Tian,Xijie Huang,Weiqi Gai,Mo Zhu,Xin Zhou,Yuze Wu,Fei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于鸟瞰图（BEV）作为全局先验的地面/近地面机器人导航系统。该系统利用图像生成模型从自然语言理解人类意图、识别目标并生成可通行性掩码，通过跨视图定位对齐机器人里程计与BEV地图以抑制长期漂移。在无人机平台上仅使用传统局部运动规划器成功完成了160米户外长距离导航任务，证明了基础模型的世界理解能力可迁移至具身导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BEV图像能为导航提供有价值的全局先验信息，但存在两个关键挑战：如何充分利用全局信息，以及如何在执行过程中可靠地使用这些信息。

Method: 1) 使用图像生成模型从自然语言理解人类意图并识别目标目的地；2) 生成可通行性掩码；3) 引入跨视图定位方法，将机器人里程计与BEV地图对齐，以减轻常规里程计的长期漂移。

Result: 在无人机平台上仅使用传统局部运动规划器，成功完成了160米户外长距离导航任务。通过大量基准实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 证明了基础模型的世界理解能力可以迁移到具身导航任务中，使机器人能够受益于现有图像生成模型的强泛化能力。

Abstract: Bird's-eye-view (BEV) images have been widely demonstrated to provide valuable prior information for navigation. Given the global information provided by such views, two key challenges remain: how to fully exploit this information and how to reliably use it during execution. In this paper, we propose a navigation system that uses BEV images as global priors and is designed for ground and near-ground robotic platforms. The system employs an image generation model to interpret human intent from natural language, identify the target destination, and generate traversability masks. During execution, we introduce cross-view localization to align the robot's odometry with the BEV map and mitigate long-term drift in conventional odometry. We conduct extensive benchmark experiments to evaluate the proposed method and further validate it on a UAV platform. Using only a conventional local motion planner, the UAV successfully completes a 160-meter outdoor long-range navigation task. This work demonstrates how the world-understanding capabilities of foundation models can be transferred to embodied navigation, enabling robots to benefit from the strong generalization ability of existing image generation models.

</details>


### [280] [Is the Future Compatible? Diagnosing Dynamic Consistency in World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07514)
*Bo-Kai Ruan,Teng-Fang Hsiao,Ling Lo,Hong-Han Shuai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出动作-状态一致性作为评估世界动作模型（WAM）可靠性的新维度，发现其能区分成功与失败的推演轨迹，并基于此提出无需额外训练的价值无关共识策略用于测试时选择，在RoboCasa和RoboTwin 2.0上提升了成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAM生成的未来推演仅评估视觉合理性，但忽略了预测动作与状态变迁之间的动态一致性，缺乏对推演轨迹决策相关可靠性的系统评估。

Method: 系统研究联合预测模型和逆动力学模型，定义动作-状态一致性指标；识别背景坍塌作为边界条件；提出基于预测未来间一致性的价值无关共识策略进行测试时选择。

Result: 动作-状态一致性有效区分成功与失败的推演轨迹，其趋势与学习到的价值估计相似；背景坍塌导致低动态失败轨迹具有欺骗性一致性；共识策略在RoboCasa和RoboTwin 2.0上提升了成功率。

Conclusion: 动作-状态一致性既是诊断WAM可靠性的有效工具，也是实现无价值函数规划的实用信号。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) enable decision-making through imagined rollouts by predicting future observations and actions. However, the reliability of these imagined futures remains under-examined: is a generated future merely visually plausible, or is it dynamically compatible with the action sequence it claims to model? In this work, we identify action-state consistency, the alignment between predicted actions and induced state transitions, as a missing reliability axis for WAMs. Through a systematic study across representative joint-prediction and inverse-dynamics models, we find that action-state consistency systematically separates successful and failed rollouts across many tasks and follows similar success-failure trends as learned value estimates. These results suggest that consistency captures decision-relevant structure beyond visual realism. We further identify background collapse as an important boundary condition, where low-dynamics failed trajectories can become deceptively consistent because static futures are easier to predict. Building on these findings, we introduce a value-free consensus strategy for test-time selection, which ranks candidate rollouts by agreement among predicted futures. This strategy improves success rates on RoboCasa and RoboTwin 2.0 without additional training or reward modeling. Taken together, our findings establish action-state consistency as both a diagnostic tool for evaluating WAM reliability and a practical signal for value-free planning.

</details>


### [281] [Search-based Robustness Testing of Laptop Refurbishing Robotic Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07530)
*Erblin Isaku,Hassan Sartaj,Shaukat Ali,Malaika Din Hashmi,Francois Picard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PROBE方法，一种基于多目标优化的搜索式鲁棒性测试方法，用于检测笔记本翻新机器人物体检测模型中的故障，该方法通过NSGA-II算法在最小扰动下有效暴露模型缺陷，效果比随机搜索好3-7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确保笔记本翻新机器人的物体检测模型对微小输入变化的鲁棒性，避免因模型故障导致翻修过程中对笔记本电脑造成损坏，支持循环经济和电子垃圾减少。

Method: 提出PROBE方法，基于NSGA-II多目标优化算法，在最小局部化扰动空间中系统搜索，同时优化故障诱导（定位与置信度）和扰动幅度两个目标，并利用蜕变关系评估非故障情况下的模型稳定性。

Result: PROBE生成故障诱导扰动的效果比随机搜索高3-7倍，所需扰动幅度更小，且生成的扰动可在不同模型间迁移；蜕变关系能提供模型鲁棒性的额外洞察。

Conclusion: PROBE是一种有效的物体检测模型鲁棒性测试方法，能够以更小扰动高效暴露故障，适用于笔记本翻新机器人等关键应用场景。

Abstract: The Danish Technological Institute (DTI) focuses on transferring advanced technologies (including robots) to the industry and the public sector. One key application is laptop refurbishment using specialized robots, aimed at promoting reuse, reducing electronic waste, and supporting the European Circular Economy Action Plan. The software of such robots often includes features that use object detection models to detect objects for various purposes, such as identifying screws for laptop disassembly or detecting stickers to remove them. Ensuring the robustness of such models to small input variations remains a critical challenge, and addressing it is important to avoid potential damage to laptops during refurbishment. In this paper, we propose PROBE, a search-based robustness testing approach that leverages multi-objective optimization to identify minimal, localized perturbations that expose failures in object detection models used in the software of laptop refurbishing robots. PROBE employs NSGA-II to systematically explore the perturbation space, optimizing for failure induction considering both localization and confidence, and perturbation magnitude, while enabling the discovery of diverse failure cases. Results show that PROBE is 3$\times$ to 7$\times$ more effective than random search in generating failure-inducing perturbations, while requiring smaller perturbation magnitudes, and that the generated perturbations transfer across models. We further show that metamorphic relations provide additional insights into model robustness, enabling the assessment of stability even in non-failing cases.

</details>


### [282] [How to utilize failure demo data?: Effective data selection for imitation learning using distribution differences in attention mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07560)
*Kana Miyamoto,Kanata Suzuki,Tetsuya Ogata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用失败数据改进机器人模仿学习的方法，通过学习成功-失败差异的潜在表征并将其融入注意力机制，同时引入后训练度量指标筛选有益的失败样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人模仿学习主要依赖成功演示训练策略，但人类数据收集过程中失败不可避免。现有利用失败数据的方法需要额外数据处理或自主 rollout 迭代更新，难以直接稳定地利用收集到的失败数据。

Method: 1) 学习成功-失败差异的潜在表征并融入注意力机制；2) 推理时从初始观测中选择适当的潜在模式以提高动作稳定性；3) 引入后训练度量指标，量化每个失败样本与成功演示之间的注意力差异，用于筛选失败数据。

Result: 仿真实验表明，所提方法在使用失败数据训练时提高了任务成功率；提出的度量指标能够识别出与成功演示结合时有益于学习的失败样本。

Conclusion: 该方法能够更高效地利用机器人数据收集流程中收集到的演示数据。

Abstract: Imitation learning for robotic tasks has relied primarily on policies trained only on successful demonstrations, although failures are unavoidable during human data collection. Many existing approaches for exploiting failure data require additional data processing or iterative policy updates through autonomous rollouts, making it difficult to directly and stably utilize failure data accumulated during data collection. In this work, we propose a method that learns latent representations of success-failure discrepancies and incorporates them into the attention mechanism. During inference, an appropriate latent mode is selected from the initial observation to improve action stability. Furthermore, we introduce a post-training metric that quantifies the attention discrepancy between each failure sample and successful demonstrations to select failure data. Simulation results show that the proposed method improves task success rates when trained with failure data and that the proposed metric identifies failure samples that are beneficial for learning when combined with successful demonstrations. These results suggest that the proposed method can support more efficient use of collected demonstrations in robotic data collection pipelines.

</details>


### [283] [MemCompiler: Compile, Don't Inject -- State-Conditioned Memory for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07594)
*Xin Ding,Xinrui Wang,Yifan Yang,Hao Wu,Shiqi Jiang,Qianxi Zhang,Liang Mi,Hanxin Zhu,Kun Li,Yunxin Liu,Zhibo Chen,Ting Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MemCompiler，通过状态条件记忆编译替代传统的提前注入静态记忆范式，动态选择和编译与当前状态相关的记忆，通过文本和潜在Soft-Mem双通道传递，在多个具身智能基准上提升性能并降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体的记忆系统采用提前注入静态记忆的方式（AMMI），这种静态设计会迅速与智能体不断变化的状态脱节，甚至导致轻量级执行器的性能低于无记忆基线。

Method: 提出MemCompiler框架，将记忆利用重塑为状态条件记忆编译：学习一个记忆编译器，读取包含智能体当前执行状态的结构化Brief State，动态选择并编译相关记忆为可执行的指导信息，通过文本通道和保留感知信息的潜在Soft-Mem通道传递。

Result: 在Alf World、EmbodiedBench和ScienceWorld三个基准上，MemCompiler相比无记忆基线在开源骨干模型上持续提升（最高+129%），匹配或接近前沿闭源系统，并将每步延迟降低60%。

Conclusion: 状态感知的记忆编译能够同时提升具身智能体的有效性和效率，静态记忆注入范式存在根本性缺陷，动态条件记忆编译是更优的替代方案。

Abstract: Existing memory systems for embodied agents typically inject retrieved memory as static context at episode start, a paradigm we term Ahead-of-time Monolithic Memory Injection (AMMI). However, this static design quickly becomes misaligned with the agent's evolving state and may degrade lightweight executors below the no-memory baseline. To address this, we propose MemCompiler, which reframes memory utilization as State-Conditioned Memory Compilation. A learned Memory Compiler reads a structured Brief State capturing the agent's current execution state and dynamically selects and compiles only relevant memory into executable guidance. This guidance is delivered through a text channel and a latent Soft-Mem channel that preserves perceptual information not expressible in text. Across Alf World, EmbodiedBench, and ScienceWorld, MemCompiler consistently improves over no-memory across open-source backbones (up to +129%), matches or approaches frontier closed-source systems, and reduces per-step latency by 60%, demonstrating that state-aware memory compilation improves both effectiveness and efficiency.

</details>


### [284] [BrickCraft: Visuomotor Skill Composition with Situated Manual Guidance for Long-Horizon Interlocking Brick Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07605)
*Jichuan Yu,Bowei Li,Zhenran Tang,Guanxing Lu,Chuxiong Hu,Ruixuan Liu,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BrickCraft是一个面向长 horizon、可泛化的互锁积木自主装配组合框架，通过相对公式化建模、情境手册引导和组合执行流水线，实现从少量演示中学习装配技能并泛化到未见结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人装配互锁积木需融合长时序任务推理、空间定位和精细操作，现有方法在这三方面的集成存在挑战，亟需一个统一框架解决。

Method: 采用相对公式化建模，将每一步锚定到局部结构中的参考积木，将复杂任务分解为有限可重用原始技能；通过情境手册（situated manuals）将装配意图投影到实时机器人观测中，弥合高层规划与物理执行间的鸿沟；采用组合式执行流水线链接空间定位技能完成长时序任务。

Result: 从有限演示中习得熟练装配技能，并展现出对未见结构的强组合泛化能力。

Conclusion: BrickCraft通过组合式框架有效解决了长时序积木装配中的推理、空间定位与精细操作集成问题，具有样本高效和泛化能力强的优势。

Abstract: Autonomous robotic assembly of interlocking bricks demands seamless integration of long-horizon task reasoning, spatial grounding, and fine-grained manipulation. This paper presents BrickCraft, a compositional framework designed for long-horizon and generalizable interlocking brick assembly. BrickCraft models the assembly process using a relative formulation, where each step is anchored to a reference brick within the partial structure, thereby decomposing complex tasks into a finite set of reusable primitive skills. BrickCraft bridges the gap between high-level assembly plans and physical execution through situated manuals, which provide explicit spatial guidance for learned visuomotor skills by projecting the assembly intent onto real-time robot observations. Finally, BrickCraft employs a compositional execution pipeline that chains these spatially grounded skills to accomplish long-horizon assembly tasks. Extensive experimental validations demonstrate that BrickCraft acquires proficient assembly skills from a limited set of demonstrations and exhibits strong compositional generalization to unseen structures. The project website is available at https://intelligent-control-lab.github.io/BrickCraft.

</details>


### [285] [PhySPRING: Structure-Preserving Reduction of Physics-Informed Twins via GNN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07687)
*Yixiong Jing,Xingyuan Chen,Guangming Wang,Olaf Wysocki,Haibing Wu,Brian Sheil*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhySPRING是一种基于可微GNN的方法，用于简化弹簧-质量数字孪生系统的拓扑结构，通过联合学习层级化粗化图拓扑及其力学参数，在保持物理与视觉保真度的同时实现高达2.30倍的加速，并在机器人策略评估中保持任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于物理的数字孪生方法在重建时往往继承视觉重建的分辨率，而非匹配任务相关的物理复杂度，导致冗余拓扑使得正向动力学 rollout 计算开销过大。

Method: 提出PhySPRING，一种全可微分的GNN方法，联合学习层级化粗化图拓扑和力学参数，通过合并具有相似动力响应的节点来优化拓扑，并保持每层为显式弹簧-质量系统。

Result: 在PhysTwin基准上，PhySPRING提升了密集重建与预测精度，简化模型保持稳定物理与视觉保真度，速度提升达2.30倍；在Real2Sim机器人策略评估中，简化模型零样本替换到ACT和π₀评估中，保持可比的操作成功率并提升动作采样效率。

Conclusion: PhySPRING实现了高效且保持结构特性的弹簧-质量系统简化，在不牺牲保真度或机器人实用性的前提下提升了计算效率。

Abstract: Physics-based digital twins aim to predict the dynamics of real-world objects under interaction, enabling real-to-sim-to-real applications in robotics. Current approaches reconstruct such twins as explicit physical models (such as spring-mass systems) to predict the dynamics, but the resulting models often inherit the resolution of the visual reconstruction rather than being reduced to the physical complexity required to reproduce task-relevant dynamics. This mismatch introduces redundant topology, making repeated forward-dynamics rollouts unnecessarily expensive. To address this challenge, we present PhySPRING, an fully differentiable GNN-based method to reduce complexity in spring--mass digital twins. PhySPRING jointly learns a hierarchy of coarsened graph topologies and their mechanical parameters from observations. At each reduction level, PhySPRING merges nodes with similar learned dynamic responses to optimize the topology, while maintaining every reduced layer as an explicit spring--mass system. On the PhysTwin benchmark, PhySPRING improves dense reconstruction and prediction accuracy over PhysTwin, while reduced models retain stable physical and visual fidelity with up to a 2.30 times speed-up. We further demonstrate the effectiveness of PhySPRING in a Real2Sim robot policy-evaluation pipeline, where the reduced models are substituted zero-shot into ACT and $π_0$ evaluations, maintaining comparable manipulation success rates across downsampling levels while improving action-sampling effectiveness. Together, PhySPRING enables efficient and structure-preserving spring--mass reduction without sacrificing fidelity or robotic utility.

</details>


### [286] [Offline-Online Hierarchical 3D Global Relocalization With Synthetic LiDAR Sensing and Descriptor-Space Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07741)
*Jiahua Ren,Kai Shen,Muhua Zhang,Lei Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种离线-在线分层框架用于3D全局重定位，通过解耦搜索空间，在离线阶段生成候选位置及几何描述子索引，在线阶段先全局检索粗估计再点云配准精估计，在3D环境中实现平均3秒重定位时间和8厘米精度，相比现有方法计算效率提升一个数量级且精度相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D全局重定位方法在大规模空间中因搜索空间庞大和计算开销高，导致在线重定位时间过长。

Method: 提出离线-在线分层框架：离线阶段在网格地图中模拟LiDAR扫描生成候选位置及其几何描述子索引；在线阶段先通过全局检索获得粗位姿估计，再通过点云配准输出精确6-DoF位姿估计。

Result: 在3D环境中平均重定位时间3秒，平均定位精度8厘米，相比现有方法计算效率提升一个数量级，重定位精度相当。

Conclusion: 该分层框架通过离线-在线解耦搜索空间，有效提升了大规模3D全局重定位的计算效率，同时保持了可比的定位精度。

Abstract: 3D global relocalization is one of the key capabilities for mobile robots in practical applications. However, in large scale spaces, existing methods often suffer from prolonged online relocalization time due to factors such as the massive pose search space and high computational overhead. To address these issues, this paper proposes an offline-online hierarchical framework that decouples the search space. In the offline phase, candidate positions and their corresponding geometric descriptor indices are generated in the map by simulating LiDAR scans within the grid map. In the online phase, a coarse pose estimate is first obtained via global retrieval, followed by point cloud registration to output precise 6-DoF pose estimates. Real-world experiments demonstrate that the proposed method achieves an average relocalization time of 3 s and an average localization accuracy of 8 cm in 3D environments. Compared with existing global relocalization methods, the proposed method achieves an order-of-magnitude improvement in computational efficiency while delivering comparable relocalization accuracy.

</details>


### [287] [Sensitivity-Based Robust NMPC for Close-Proximity Offshore Wind Turbine Inspection with a Tilted Multirotor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07771)
*Giuseppe Silano,Martin Saska*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于灵敏度的鲁棒NMPC方法，通过在线约束收紧处理倾斜多旋翼在近海风力涡轮机检测中的塔架净空约束问题，在500次蒙特卡洛仿真中消除了名义NMPC的净空违规，仅轻微增加求解时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在近海风力涡轮机近距离检测任务中，大型圆柱结构周围的净空控制要求严格，而名义NMPC在质量、惯性、推力效率、阻力或风况偏离名义假设时可能违反安全约束，需要一种鲁棒控制方法保证安全。

Method: 提出灵敏度鲁棒NMPC：利用一阶参数状态灵敏度提供结构化不确定性裕度，结合阶段相关附加裕度处理阵风，通过在线约束收紧增强塔架净空约束的鲁棒性；保留名义NMPC的滚动时域优化结构不变，仅增加灵敏度传播和裕度评估模块。

Result: 在边界关键的螺旋检测轨迹上进行了500次不确定性实现的蒙特卡洛评估，结果表明所提控制器消除了名义NMPC下的净空违规，求解时间仅有适度增加。

Conclusion: 基于灵敏度的鲁棒NMPC能有效处理参数不确定性和风扰动，在保证安全性的同时保持计算效率，适用于近海风力涡轮机检测等需严格净空控制的任务。

Abstract: Close-proximity offshore wind turbine inspection requires strict clearance control around large cylindrical structures under wind and model mismatch. Nominal Nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC) may violate safety constraints when mass, inertia, thrust effectiveness, drag, or wind conditions differ from nominal assumptions. We propose a sensitivity-based robust NMPC for a tilted multirotor that robustifies the tower-clearance constraint via online constraint tightening. First-order parametric state sensitivities provide a structured-uncertainty margin, while bounded gusts are handled by a stage-dependent additive margin. The formulation augments the nominal NMPC with sensitivity propagation and margin evaluation only, leaving the receding-horizon optimization structure unchanged. Monte-Carlo evaluation over 500 uncertainty realizations on a boundary-critical helical inspection trajectory shows that the proposed controller eliminates the clearance violations observed under nominal NMPC at the cost of a moderate increase in solve time.

</details>


### [288] [Many-to-Many Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07835)
*Ethan Schneider,Jingkai Chen,Tianyi Gu,Kunlei Lian,Seth Hutchinson,Sonia Chernova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对多对多仓库场景下的多智能体拣选与配送问题（MAPD），本文提出M2M算法，将SKU分布纳入优化目标，在模拟8小时仓库运营中平均完成最多22,000个额外任务，持续达到或超越现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAPD研究多聚焦一对一任务（固定取送货点），而实际仓库中物品（通过SKU标识）可存放于多个位置，形成NP难的四维分配问题，现有方法无法有效解决多对多场景。

Method: 提出M2M算法，包含两个变体：M2M（最小化估计任务时长）和M2M-wSKU（将SKU分布纳入目标函数）。通过模拟8小时仓库运营在不同环境和库存密度下进行验证。

Result: M2M算法持续达到或超越现有最优方法，平均完成最多22,000个额外任务。

Conclusion: M2M算法能有效解决多对多MAPD问题，通过将SKU分布纳入优化可进一步提升仓库运营效率。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems in automated warehouses must manage continuous streams of pickup-and-delivery tasks while ensuring efficiency and safety. Prior work on Multi-Agent Pickup-and-Delivery (MAPD) has largely focused on the one-to-one variant, where each task has a fixed pickup and delivery location. In contrast, real warehouses often present many-to-many MAPD scenarios, where items, tracked by stock keeping unit (SKU) identifiers, can be retrieved from or stored at multiple locations, resulting in an NP-hard four-dimensional assignment problem. To solve the many-to-many MAPD problem, we contribute our algorithm: Many-to-Many Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery (M2M). We experiment with two variants of our algorithm: one that minimizes estimated task durations (M2M), and one which incorporates SKU distribution into the objective function (M2M-wSKU). Simulation results over 8-hour warehouse operations show that our method consistently matches or outperforms prior state of the art, with M2M completing up to 22,000 more tasks on average across different environments and warehouse inventory densities.

</details>


### [289] [Melding LLM and temporal logic for reliable human-swarm collaboration in complex scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07877)
*Junfeng Chen,Yuxiao Zhu,An Zhuo,Xintong Zhang,Shuo Zhang,Guanghui Wen,Xiwang Dong,Meng Guo,Zhongkui Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经符号框架，通过结合时态逻辑约束、任务自动机、LLM推理和不确定性感知调度，实现了动态环境下长期人-蜂群协作的可靠任务规划，显著降低了操作员的认知负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的规划工具在长期、动态场景中容易产生无效任务排序和不可行动作，需要频繁人工调整；持续操作员监控带来巨大认知负担，亟需一种可靠且低开销的协作范式。

Method: 1) 将任务目标和操作规则形式化为时态逻辑公式，将可行任务排序形式化为任务自动机；2) LLM在形式约束和实时感知上下文中生成可执行的子任务序列；3) 不确定性感知调度器在异构蜂群中分配子任务以最大化并行性并抵抗干扰；4) 事件触发交互协议将操作员参与限制在稀疏的高层确认和指导。

Result: 在异构机器人车队上的部署显示，该方法在保持鲁棒性的同时产生了相似的结果，能够应对硬件特定的执行和通信不确定性。

Conclusion: 该工作为动态环境中可靠且低开销的人-蜂群协作提供了形式化且可扩展的范式。

Abstract: Robot swarms promise scalable assistance in complex and hazardous environments. Task planning lies at the core of human-swarm collaboration, translating the operator's intent into coordinated swarm actions and helping determine when validation or intervention is required during execution. In long-horizon missions under dynamic scenarios, however, reliable task planning becomes difficult to maintain: emerging events and changing conditions demand continual adaptation, and sustained operator oversight imposes substantial cognitive burden. Existing LLM-based planning tools can support plan generation, yet they remain susceptible to invalid task orderings and infeasible robot actions, resulting in frequent manual adjustment. Here we introduce a neuro-symbolic framework for long-horizon human-swarm collaboration that tightly melds verifiable task planning with context-grounded LLM reasoning. We formalize mission goals and operational rules as temporal logic formulas and admissible task orderings as task automata. Conditioned on these formal constraints and live perceptual context, LLMs generate executable subtask sequences that satisfy mission rules and remain grounded in the current scene. An uncertainty-aware scheduler then assigns subtasks across the heterogeneous swarm to maximize parallelisms while remaining resilient to disruptions. An event-triggered interaction protocol further limits operator involvement to sparse, high-level confirmation and guidance. Deployment on a heterogeneous robotic fleet yields similar results while remaining robust to hardware-specific actuation and communication uncertainties. Together, these results support a formal and scalable paradigm for reliable and low-overhead human-swarm collaboration in dynamic environments

</details>


### [290] [AERO-VIS: Asynchronous Event-based Real-time Onboard Visual-Inertial SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07885)
*Yannick Burkhardt,Sebastián Barbas Laina,Simon Boche,Leonard Freißmuth,Stefan Leutenegger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AERO-VIS是一个立体事件-惯性SLAM系统，采用异步事件流处理和数据驱动的关键点检测器，首次实现了纯事件基础的闭环无人机控制和大规模状态估计，在机载计算上达到前所未有的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机虽具高动态范围和高时间分辨率优势，但现有事件里程计方法仍采用固定速率同步处理，限制了延迟和吞吐量，未能充分发挥事件相机的异步特性。

Method: 提出AERO-VIS系统，包含：(1) 数据驱动的鲁棒关键点检测器；(2) 异步事件流处理机制，动态适应下游运行时需求；(3) 立体事件-惯性SLAM框架，在机载计算上实现闭环控制。

Result: 在无人机上部署时，AERO-VIS在机载事件SLAM中达到了前所未有的精度，并首次展示了纯事件基础的闭环无人机控制和大规模状态估计。

Conclusion: 异步事件处理结合数据驱动关键点检测可显著提升事件SLAM系统的延迟、吞吐量和精度，为事件相机在机器人领域的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The robustness of event cameras to high dynamic range and motion blur holds the potential to improve visual odometry systems in challenging environments. Although their high temporal resolution does not require synchronous processing, most event-based odometry methods still run at fixed rates, which simplifies system design but restricts latency and throughput. In this work, we present AERO-VIS, a stereo event-inertial SLAM system with an integrated, data-driven, robust, and performance-optimized keypoint detector. By processing the event stream asynchronously, the system dynamically adapts to downstream runtime demands, ensuring low-latency and real-time performance. When deploying AERO-VIS on a UAV, we achieve unprecedented accuracy in onboard event-based SLAM. These unique characteristics enable us to present the first purely event-based inertial SLAM system that demonstrates closed-loop UAV control and large-scale state estimation while relying solely on onboard compute. A video of the experiments and the source code are available at ethz-mrl.github.io/AERO-VIS.

</details>


### [291] [TAVIS: A Benchmark for Egocentric Active Vision and Anticipatory Gaze in Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07943)
*Giacomo Spigler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TAVIS是首个面向主动视觉模仿学习的标准化评估平台，包含头戴式(5任务)和手部(3任务)两个任务套件，基于两种人形躯干在IsaacLab上构建。它提供了头戴vs固定相机对比协议、GALT凝视提前时间指标以及ID/OOD分布划分等评估原语。实验发现主动视觉的帮助是任务条件性的、多任务策略在分布偏移下性能急剧下降、模仿学习可自主产生预期性凝视。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动视觉在模仿学习中已被多个独立系统证明有效，但缺乏统一的基准来比较不同方法、量化主动视觉的贡献及其适用的任务类型和条件。TAVIS旨在填补这一空白，提供标准化的评估基础设施。

Method: 构建了两个互补任务套件(TAVIS-Head 5任务和TAVIS-Hands 3任务)在两种人形躯干(GR1T2, Reachy2)上，基于IsaacLab。提供三种评估原语：(1)头戴相机vs固定相机配对协议；(2)GALT(Gaze-Action Lead Time)指标量化预期性凝视；(3)程序化ID/OOD分布划分。使用Diffusion Policy和π₀作为基线。

Result: (i)主动视觉总体上有帮助，但效益是任务条件性的而非均匀的；(ii)多任务策略在两种任务套件的控制分布偏移下性能急剧下降；(iii)仅凭模仿学习即可产生预期性凝视，中位提前时间可与人类遥操作参考相媲美。
